+368|
+369|        html += `<div class="patchpad-caret${isPrimary ? ' primary' : ''}" 
style="top:${top}px;left:${left}px;height:${this.lineHeight}px;"></div>`;
+370|      }
+371|      this.caretsLayer.innerHTML = html;
+372|    }
+373|
+374|    _resetCaretBlink() {
+375|      const carets = this.caretsLayer.querySelectorAll('.patchpad-caret');
+376|      carets.forEach(c => {
+377|        c.style.animation = 'none';
+378|        // force reflow
+379|        void c.offsetWidth;
+380|        c.style.animation = '';
+381|      });
+382|    }
+383|
+384|    _notifyCursorChange() {
+385|      const p = this.selectionManager.primary.head;
+386|      const lineText = this.doc.getLine(p.row);
+387|      const graphemes = Grapheme.getGraphemes(lineText.substring(0, 
p.col));
+388|
+389|      if (this.options.onCursorChange) {
+390|        this.options.onCursorChange({
+391|          row: p.row,
+392|          col: p.col,
+393|          lineNumber: p.row + 1,
+394|          columnNumber: graphemes.length + 1,
+395|          caretCount: this.selectionManager.selections.length,
+396|          selection: this.selectionManager.primary,
+397|          totalLines: this.doc.lineCount,
+398|          totalChars: this.doc.charCount
+399|        });
+400|      }
+401|    }
+402|
+403|    // --- Document & Editing Actions ---
+404|
+405|    insertText(text, isTyping = false) {
+406|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+407|      const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+408|      const beforeSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+409|
+410|      // Sort selections descending so lower positions don't shift higher 
positions
+411|      const sortedIndexes = this.selectionManager.selections
+412|        .map((s, idx) => ({ sel: s, idx }))
+413|        .sort((a, b) => {
+414|          const rA = a.sel.getRange();
+415|          const rB = b.sel.getRange();
+416|          if (rA.start.row !== rB.start.row) return rB.start.row - 
rA.start.row;
+417|          return rB.start.col - rA.start.col;
+418|        });
+419|
+420|      const newSelections = new 
Array(this.selectionManager.selections.length);
+421|
+422|      // Check if multiline paste with exact caret count match
+423|      const pastedLines = text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/);
+424|      const isMultiLinePerCaret = !isTyping && pastedLines.length > 1 && 
pastedLines.length === sortedIndexes.length;
+425|
+426|      for (let i = 0; i < sortedIndexes.length; i++) {
+427|        const item = sortedIndexes[i];
+428|        const sel = item.sel;
+429|        const textToInsert = isMultiLinePerCaret ? pastedLines[item.idx] : 
text;
+430|
+431|        if (!sel.isEmpty()) {
+432|          const range = sel.getRange();
+433|          this.doc.deleteRange(range.start, range.end);
+434|          const res = this.doc.insertAt(range.start, textToInsert);
+435|          newSelections[item.idx] = new Selection({ row: res.newRow, col: 
res.newCol }, { row: res.newRow, col: res.newCol });
+436|        } else {
+437|          const res = this.doc.insertAt(sel.head, textToInsert);
+438|          newSelections[item.idx] = new Selection({ row: res.newRow, col: 
res.newCol }, { row: res.newRow, col: res.newCol });
+439|        }
+440|      }
+441|
+442|      this.selectionManager.selections = newSelections;
+443|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+444|
+445|      const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+446|      const afterSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+447|
+448|      this.history.recordChange({
+449|        beforeText,
+450|        afterText,
+451|        beforeSelections,
+452|        afterSelections,
+453|        actionType: isTyping && text.length === 1 ? 'type_char' : 'insert',
+454|        caretPos: this.selectionManager.primary.head
+455|      });
+456|
+457|      this.render();
+458|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+459|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+460|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+461|      if (this.options.onDocChange) {
+462|        this.options.onDocChange(afterText);
+463|      }
+464|    }
+465|
+466|    deleteBackward() {
+467|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+468|      const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+469|      const beforeSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+470|
+471|      const sortedIndexes = this.selectionManager.selections
+472|        .map((s, idx) => ({ sel: s, idx }))
+473|        .sort((a, b) => {
+474|          const rA = a.sel.getRange();
+475|          const rB = b.sel.getRange();
+476|          if (rA.start.row !== rB.start.row) return rB.start.row - 
rA.start.row;
+477|          return rB.start.col - rA.start.col;
+478|        });
+479|
+480|      const newSelections = new 
Array(this.selectionManager.selections.length);
+481|
+482|      for (let i = 0; i < sortedIndexes.length; i++) {
+483|        const item = sortedIndexes[i];
+484|        const sel = item.sel;
+485|
+486|        if (!sel.isEmpty()) {
+487|          const range = sel.getRange();
+488|          const res = this.doc.deleteRange(range.start, range.end);
+489|          newSelections[item.idx] = new Selection(res.newPos, res.newPos);
+490|        } else {
+491|          const pos = sel.head;
+492|          if (pos.col > 0) {
+493|            const lineText = this.doc.getLine(pos.row);
+494|            const prevCol = Grapheme.prevGraphemeCol(lineText, pos.col);
+495|            const res = this.doc.deleteRange({ row: pos.row, col: prevCol 
}, pos);
+496|            newSelections[item.idx] = new Selection(res.newPos, 
res.newPos);
+497|          } else if (pos.row > 0) {
+498|            const prevRow = pos.row - 1;
+499|            const prevLineLen = this.doc.getLine(prevRow).length;
+500|            const res = this.doc.deleteRange({ row: prevRow, col: 
prevLineLen }, pos);
+501|            newSelections[item.idx] = new Selection(res.newPos, 
res.newPos);
+502|          } else {
+503|            newSelections[item.idx] = sel;
+504|          }
+505|        }
+506|      }
+507|
+508|      this.selectionManager.selections = newSelections;
+509|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+510|
+511|      const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+512|      const afterSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+513|
+514|      this.history.recordChange({
+515|        beforeText,
+516|        afterText,
+517|        beforeSelections,
+518|        afterSelections,
+519|        actionType: 'delete_back',
+520|        caretPos: this.selectionManager.primary.head
+521|      });
+522|
+523|      this.render();
+524|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+525|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+526|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+527|      if (this.options.onDocChange) {
+528|        this.options.onDocChange(afterText);
+529|      }
+530|    }
+531|
+532|    deleteForward() {
+533|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+534|      const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+535|      const beforeSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+536|
+537|      const sortedIndexes = this.selectionManager.selections
+538|        .map((s, idx) => ({ sel: s, idx }))
+539|        .sort((a, b) => {
+540|          const rA = a.sel.getRange();
+541|          const rB = b.sel.getRange();
+542|          if (rA.start.row !== rB.start.row) return rB.start.row - 
rA.start.row;
+543|          return rB.start.col - rA.start.col;
+544|        });
+545|
+546|      const newSelections = new 
Array(this.selectionManager.selections.length);
+547|
+548|      for (let i = 0; i < sortedIndexes.length; i++) {
+549|        const item = sortedIndexes[i];
+550|        const sel = item.sel;
+551|
+552|        if (!sel.isEmpty()) {
+553|          const range = sel.getRange();
+554|          const res = this.doc.deleteRange(range.start, range.end);
+555|          newSelections[item.idx] = new Selection(res.newPos, res.newPos);
+556|        } else {
+557|          const pos = sel.head;
+558|          const lineText = this.doc.getLine(pos.row);
+559|          if (pos.col < lineText.length) {
+560|            const nextCol = Grapheme.nextGraphemeCol(lineText, pos.col);
+561|            const res = this.doc.deleteRange(pos, { row: pos.row, col: 
nextCol });
+562|            newSelections[item.idx] = new Selection(res.newPos, 
res.newPos);
+563|          } else if (pos.row < this.doc.lineCount - 1) {
+564|            const res = this.doc.deleteRange(pos, { row: pos.row + 1, col: 
0 });
+565|            newSelections[item.idx] = new Selection(res.newPos, 
res.newPos);
+566|          } else {
+567|            newSelections[item.idx] = sel;
+568|          }
+569|        }
+570|      }
+571|
+572|      this.selectionManager.selections = newSelections;
+573|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+574|
+575|      const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+576|      const afterSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+577|
+578|      this.history.recordChange({
+579|        beforeText,
+580|        afterText,
+581|        beforeSelections,
+582|        afterSelections,
+583|        actionType: 'delete_forward',
+584|        caretPos: this.selectionManager.primary.head
+585|      });
+586|
+587|      this.render();
+588|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+589|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+590|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+591|      if (this.options.onDocChange) {
+592|        this.options.onDocChange(afterText);
+593|      }
+594|    }
+595|
+596|    insertNewline() {
+597|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+598|      // Extract leading indent from primary line for auto-indent
+599|      const p = this.selectionManager.primary.head;
+600|      const currentLine = this.doc.getLine(p.row);
+601|      const matchIndent = currentLine.match(/^\s*/);
+602|      const indent = matchIndent ? matchIndent[0] : '';
+603|
+604|      this.insertText('\n' + indent, false);
+605|    }
+606|
+607|    indent() {
+608|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+609|      // If single collapsed selection, insert spaces
+610|      if (this.selectionManager.selections.length === 1 && 
this.selectionManager.primary.isEmpty()) {
+611|        this.insertText('  ', false);
+612|        return;
+613|      }
+614|
+615|      const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+616|      const beforeSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+617|
+618|      for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+619|        const range = sel.getRange();
+620|        this.doc.indentLines(range.start.row, range.end.row, '  ');
+621|        sel.anchor.col += 2;
+622|        sel.head.col += 2;
+623|      }
+624|
+625|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+626|      const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+627|      const afterSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+628|
+629|      this.history.recordChange({
+630|        beforeText,
+631|        afterText,
+632|        beforeSelections,
+633|        afterSelections,
+634|        actionType: 'indent'
+635|      });
+636|
+637|      this.render();
+638|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+639|      if (this.options.onDocChange) {
+640|        this.options.onDocChange(afterText);
+641|      }
+642|    }
+643|
+644|    unindent() {
+645|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+646|      const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+647|      const beforeSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+648|
+649|      for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+650|        const range = sel.getRange();
+651|        const removed = this.doc.unindentLines(range.start.row, 
range.end.row, '  ');
+652|        sel.anchor.col = Math.max(0, sel.anchor.col - (removed[0] || 0));
+653|        sel.head.col = Math.max(0, sel.head.col - (removed[removed.length -
1] || 0));
+654|      }
+655|
+656|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+657|      const afterText = this.doc.getText();
+658|      const afterSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+659|
+660|      this.history.recordChange({
+661|        beforeText,
+662|        afterText,
+663|        beforeSelections,
+664|        afterSelections,
+665|        actionType: 'unindent'
+666|      });
+667|
+668|      this.render();
+669|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+670|      if (this.options.onDocChange) {
+671|        this.options.onDocChange(afterText);
+672|      }
+673|    }
+674|
+675|    undo() {
+676|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+677|      const res = this.history.undo(this.doc.getText());
+678|      if (!res) return;
+679|
+680|      this.doc.setText(res.text);
+681|      this.selectionManager.selections = res.selections;
+682|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+683|
+684|      this.render();
+685|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+686|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+687|      if (this.options.onDocChange) {
+688|        this.options.onDocChange(res.text);
+689|      }
+690|    }
+691|
+692|    redo() {
+693|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+694|      const res = this.history.redo(this.doc.getText());
+695|      if (!res) return;
+696|
+697|      this.doc.setText(res.text);
+698|      this.selectionManager.selections = res.selections;
+699|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+700|
+701|      this.render();
+702|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+703|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+704|      if (this.options.onDocChange) {
+705|        this.options.onDocChange(res.text);
+706|      }
+707|    }
+708|
+709|    restoreRevisionContent(content) {
+710|      const beforeText = this.doc.getText();
+711|      const beforeSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+712|
+713|      this.doc.setText(content);
+714|      this.selectionManager.setSingleCaret(0, 0);
+715|
+716|      const afterSelections = this.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone());
+717|
+718|      // Pushes onto undo stack so restoring can be undone!
+719|      this.history.recordChange({
+720|        beforeText,
+721|        afterText: content,
+722|        beforeSelections,
+723|        afterSelections,
+724|        actionType: 'restore'
+725|      });
+726|
+727|      this.render();
+728|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+729|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+730|      if (this.options.onDocChange) {
+731|        this.options.onDocChange(content);
+732|      }
+733|    }
+734|
+735|    // --- Cursor Navigation ---
+736|
+737|    moveLeft(extend = false, byWord = false) {
+738|      for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+739|        if (!extend && !sel.isEmpty() && !byWord) {
+740|          const range = sel.getRange();
+741|          sel.anchor = { ...range.start };
+742|          sel.head = { ...range.start };
+743|          continue;
+744|        }
+745|
+746|        let nextPos;
+747|        if (byWord) {
+748|          nextPos = this.doc.findPrevWord(sel.head.row, sel.head.col);
+749|        } else {
+750|          const lineText = this.doc.getLine(sel.head.row);
+751|          if (sel.head.col > 0) {
+752|            const prevCol = Grapheme.prevGraphemeCol(lineText, 
sel.head.col);
+753|            nextPos = { row: sel.head.row, col: prevCol };
+754|          } else if (sel.head.row > 0) {
+755|            const prevRow = sel.head.row - 1;
+756|            nextPos = { row: prevRow, col: this.doc.getLine(prevRow).length
};
+757|          } else {
+758|            nextPos = { row: 0, col: 0 };
+759|          }
+760|        }
+761|
+762|        sel.head = nextPos;
+763|        if (!extend) {
+764|          sel.anchor = { ...nextPos };
+765|        }
+766|      }
+767|
+768|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+769|      this.render();
+770|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+771|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+772|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+773|    }
+774|
+775|    moveRight(extend = false, byWord = false) {
+776|      for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+777|        if (!extend && !sel.isEmpty() && !byWord) {
+778|          const range = sel.getRange();
+779|          sel.anchor = { ...range.end };
+780|          sel.head = { ...range.end };
+781|          continue;
+782|        }
+783|
+784|        let nextPos;
+785|        if (byWord) {
+786|          nextPos = this.doc.findNextWord(sel.head.row, sel.head.col);
+787|        } else {
+788|          const lineText = this.doc.getLine(sel.head.row);
+789|          if (sel.head.col < lineText.length) {
+790|            const nextCol = Grapheme.nextGraphemeCol(lineText, 
sel.head.col);
+791|            nextPos = { row: sel.head.row, col: nextCol };
+792|          } else if (sel.head.row < this.doc.lineCount - 1) {
+793|            nextPos = { row: sel.head.row + 1, col: 0 };
+794|          } else {
+795|            nextPos = { row: sel.head.row, col: lineText.length };
+796|          }
+797|        }
+798|
+799|        sel.head = nextPos;
+800|        if (!extend) {
+801|          sel.anchor = { ...nextPos };
+802|        }
+803|      }
+804|
+805|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+806|      this.render();
+807|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+808|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+809|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+810|    }
+811|
+812|    moveUp(extend = false, numLines = 1) {
+813|      for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+814|        const targetRow = Math.max(0, sel.head.row - numLines);
+815|        const targetLine = this.doc.getLine(targetRow);
+816|        const targetCol = Grapheme.clampCol(targetLine, sel.head.col);
+817|        const nextPos = { row: targetRow, col: targetCol };
+818|
+819|        sel.head = nextPos;
+820|        if (!extend) {
+821|          sel.anchor = { ...nextPos };
+822|        }
+823|      }
+824|
+825|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+826|      this.render();
+827|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+828|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+829|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+830|    }
+831|
+832|    moveDown(extend = false, numLines = 1) {
+833|      for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+834|        const targetRow = Math.min(this.doc.lineCount - 1, sel.head.row + 
numLines);
+835|        const targetLine = this.doc.getLine(targetRow);
+836|        const targetCol = Grapheme.clampCol(targetLine, sel.head.col);
+837|        const nextPos = { row: targetRow, col: targetCol };
+838|
+839|        sel.head = nextPos;
+840|        if (!extend) {
+841|          sel.anchor = { ...nextPos };
+842|        }
+843|      }
+844|
+845|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+846|      this.render();
+847|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+848|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+849|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+850|    }
+851|
+852|    moveHome(extend = false, toDocStart = false) {
+853|      if (toDocStart) {
+854|        for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+855|          sel.head = { row: 0, col: 0 };
+856|          if (!extend) sel.anchor = { row: 0, col: 0 };
+857|        }
+858|      } else {
+859|        for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+860|          const lineText = this.doc.getLine(sel.head.row);
+861|          const firstNonWs = lineText.search(/\S/);
+862|          let targetCol = 0;
+863|          if (firstNonWs >= 0 && sel.head.col !== firstNonWs) {
+864|            targetCol = firstNonWs;
+865|          }
+866|          sel.head = { row: sel.head.row, col: targetCol };
+867|          if (!extend) sel.anchor = { row: sel.head.row, col: targetCol };
+868|        }
+869|      }
+870|
+871|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+872|      this.render();
+873|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+874|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+875|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+876|    }
+877|
+878|    moveEnd(extend = false, toDocEnd = false) {
+879|      if (toDocEnd) {
+880|        const lastRow = this.doc.lineCount - 1;
+881|        const lastCol = this.doc.getLine(lastRow).length;
+882|        for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+883|          sel.head = { row: lastRow, col: lastCol };
+884|          if (!extend) sel.anchor = { row: lastRow, col: lastCol };
+885|        }
+886|      } else {
+887|        for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+888|          const lineLen = this.doc.getLine(sel.head.row).length;
+889|          sel.head = { row: sel.head.row, col: lineLen };
+890|          if (!extend) sel.anchor = { row: sel.head.row, col: lineLen };
+891|        }
+892|      }
+893|
+894|      this.selectionManager.normalize();
+895|      this.render();
+896|      this.scrollCursorIntoView();
+897|      this._resetCaretBlink();
+898|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+899|    }
+900|
+901|    selectAll() {
+902|      this.selectionManager.selectAll(this.doc);
+903|      this.render();
+904|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+905|    }
+906|
+907|    selectLine(row) {
+908|      this.selectionManager.selectLine(this.doc, row);
+909|      this.render();
+910|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+911|    }
+912|
+913|    selectWord(row, col) {
+914|      this.selectionManager.selectWord(this.doc, row, col);
+915|      this.render();
+916|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+917|    }
+918|
+919|    selectMatch(match) {
+920|      if (!match) return;
+921|      this.selectionManager.selections = [
+922|        new Selection({ row: match.row, col: match.startCol }, { row: 
match.row, col: match.endCol })
+923|      ];
+924|      this.render();
+925|      this.scrollCursorIntoView({ row: match.row, col: match.startCol });
+926|      this._notifyCursorChange();
+927|    }
+928|
+929|    getSelectedText() {
+930|      const parts = [];
+931|      for (const sel of this.selectionManager.selections) {
+932|        if (!sel.isEmpty()) {
+933|          const range = sel.getRange();
+934|          parts.push(this.doc.getRangeText(range.start, range.end));
+935|        }
+936|      }
+937|      return parts.join('\n');
+938|    }
+939|
+940|    replaceSelection(text) {
+941|      this.insertText(text, false);
+942|    }
+943|
+944|    // --- Event Binding ---
+945|
+946|    _bindEvents() {
+947|      // Synchronize scroll between gutter and viewport
+948|      this.viewport.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
+949|        this.gutter.scrollTop = this.viewport.scrollTop;
+950|        this.render();
+951|      }, { passive: true });
+952|
+953|      // Focus / Blur
+954|      this.container.addEventListener('focus', () => {
+955|        this.isFocused = true;
+956|        this.container.classList.add('focused');
+957|        this.render();
+958|      });
+959|
+960|      this.container.addEventListener('blur', () => {
+961|        this.isFocused = false;
+962|        this.container.classList.remove('focused');
+963|        this.render();
+964|      });
+965|
+966|      // Mouse Down on viewport
+967|      this.viewport.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
+968|        if (e.button !== 0) return; // Left click only
+969|        this.container.focus();
+970|
+971|        const pos = this.posFromCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
+972|
+973|        if (e.altKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
+974|          // Multi-caret add
+975|          this.selectionManager.addCaret(pos.row, pos.col);
+976|        } else if (e.shiftKey) {
+977|          // Extend primary selection
+978|          this.selectionManager.primary.head = pos;
+979|          this.selectionManager.normalize();
+980|        } else {
+981|          // Double / Triple click
+982|          if (e.detail === 2) {
+983|            this.selectWord(pos.row, pos.col);
+984|            return;
+985|          } else if (e.detail >= 3) {
+986|            this.selectLine(pos.row);
+987|            return;
+988|          }
+989|
+990|          this.selectionManager.setSingleCaret(pos.row, pos.col);
+991|        }
+992|
+993|        this.isDragging = true;
+994|        this.dragStartPos = pos;
+995|        this.render();
+996|        this._notifyCursorChange();
+997|      });
+998|
+999|      // Window mouse move (for drag selection)
+1000|      window.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
+1001|        if (!this.isDragging) return;
+1002|
+1003|        const pos = this.posFromCoords(e.clientX, e.clientY);
+1004|        this.selectionManager.primary.head = pos;
+1005|        this.selectionManager.normalize();
+1006|        this.render();
+1007|        this._notifyCursorChange();
+1008|
+1009|        // Auto-scroll when dragging near viewport boundaries
+1010|        const rect = this.viewport.getBoundingClientRect();
+1011|        if (e.clientY < rect.top + 30) {
+1012|          this.viewport.scrollTop -= 20;
+1013|        } else if (e.clientY > rect.bottom - 30) {
+1014|          this.viewport.scrollTop += 20;
+1015|        }
+1016|      });
+1017|
+1018|      window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
+1019|        if (this.isDragging) {
+1020|          this.isDragging = false;
+1021|          this.dragStartPos = null;
+1022|        }
+1023|      });
+1024|
+1025|      // Click on gutter line number
+1026|      this.gutter.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
+1027|        const lineElem = e.target.closest('.patchpad-gutter-line');
+1028|        if (!lineElem) return;
+1029|        const row = parseInt(lineElem.getAttribute('data-row'), 10);
+1030|        if (!isNaN(row)) {
+1031|          this.container.focus();
+1032|          this.selectLine(row);
+1033|        }
+1034|      });
+1035|
+1036|      // Keyboard events
+1037|      this.container.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => 
this._handleKeyDown(e));
+1038|
+1039|      // Clipboard events
+1040|      this.container.addEventListener('copy', (e) => this._handleCopy(e));
+1041|      this.container.addEventListener('cut', (e) => this._handleCut(e));
+1042|      this.container.addEventListener('paste', (e) => 
this._handlePaste(e));
+1043|    }
+1044|
+1045|    _handleKeyDown(e) {
+1046|      const isMac = /Mac|iPod|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.platform);
+1047|      const modKey = isMac ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey;
+1048|
+1049|      // Handle shortcuts
+1050|      if (modKey && !e.shiftKey && !e.altKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 
'a') {
+1051|        e.preventDefault();
+1052|        this.selectAll();
+1053|        return;
+1054|      }
+1055|
+1056|      if (modKey && !e.shiftKey && !e.altKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 
'z') {
+1057|        e.preventDefault();
+1058|        this.undo();
+1059|        return;
+1060|      }
+1061|
+1062|      if ((modKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'y') || (modKey && e.shiftKey
&& e.key.toLowerCase() === 'z')) {
+1063|        e.preventDefault();
+1064|        this.redo();
+1065|        return;
+1066|      }
+1067|
+1068|      if (modKey && !e.shiftKey && !e.altKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 
's') {
+1069|        e.preventDefault();
+1070|        if (this.options.onSaveRequest) {
+1071|          this.options.onSaveRequest();
+1072|        }
+1073|        return;
+1074|      }
+1075|
+1076|      if (modKey && !e.altKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'f') {
+1077|        e.preventDefault();
+1078|        if (this.options.onFindRequest) {
+1079|          this.options.onFindRequest(false);
+1080|        }
+1081|        return;
+1082|      }
+1083|
+1084|      if (modKey && !e.altKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'h') {
+1085|        e.preventDefault();
+1086|        if (this.options.onFindRequest) {
+1087|          this.options.onFindRequest(true);
+1088|        }
+1089|        return;
+1090|      }
+1091|
+1092|      // Escape key
+1093|      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
+1094|        e.preventDefault();
+1095|        if (this.options.onEscape) {
+1096|          this.options.onEscape();
+1097|        }
+1098|        return;
+1099|      }
+1100|
+1101|      // Tab and Shift+Tab
+1102|      if (e.key === 'Tab') {
+1103|        e.preventDefault();
+1104|        if (e.shiftKey) {
+1105|          this.unindent();
+1106|        } else {
+1107|          this.indent();
+1108|        }
+1109|        return;
+1110|      }
+1111|
+1112|      // Navigation & editing keys
+1113|      const byWord = isMac ? e.altKey : e.ctrlKey;
+1114|      const toDocEnds = isMac ? e.metaKey : e.ctrlKey;
+1115|
+1116|      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
+1117|        e.preventDefault();
+1118|        this.moveLeft(e.shiftKey, byWord);
+1119|        return;
+1120|      }
+1121|
+1122|      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
+1123|        e.preventDefault();
+1124|        this.moveRight(e.shiftKey, byWord);
+1125|        return;
+1126|      }
+1127|
+1128|      if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
+1129|        e.preventDefault();
+1130|        if (toDocEnds) {
+1131|          this.moveHome(e.shiftKey, true);
+1132|        } else {
+1133|          this.moveUp(e.shiftKey);
+1134|        }
+1135|        return;
+1136|      }
+1137|
+1138|      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
+1139|        e.preventDefault();
+1140|        if (toDocEnds) {
+1141|          this.moveEnd(e.shiftKey, true);
+1142|        } else {
+1143|          this.moveDown(e.shiftKey);
+1144|        }
+1145|        return;
+1146|      }
+1147|
+1148|      if (e.key === 'Home') {
+1149|        e.preventDefault();
+1150|        this.moveHome(e.shiftKey, toDocEnds);
+1151|        return;
+1152|      }
+1153|
+1154|      if (e.key === 'End') {
+1155|        e.preventDefault();
+1156|        this.moveEnd(e.shiftKey, toDocEnds);
+1157|        return;
+1158|      }
+1159|
+1160|      if (e.key === 'PageUp') {
+1161|        e.preventDefault();
+1162|        const linesPerPage = Math.max(1, 
Math.floor(this.viewport.clientHeight / this.lineHeight) - 2);
+1163|        this.moveUp(e.shiftKey, linesPerPage);
+1164|        return;
+1165|      }
+1166|
+1167|      if (e.key === 'PageDown') {
+1168|        e.preventDefault();
+1169|        const linesPerPage = Math.max(1, 
Math.floor(this.viewport.clientHeight / this.lineHeight) - 2);
+1170|        this.moveDown(e.shiftKey, linesPerPage);
+1171|        return;
+1172|      }
+1173|
+1174|      if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
+1175|        e.preventDefault();
+1176|        this.deleteBackward();
+1177|        return;
+1178|      }
+1179|
+1180|      if (e.key === 'Delete') {
+1181|        e.preventDefault();
+1182|        this.deleteForward();
+1183|        return;
+1184|      }
+1185|
+1186|      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
+1187|        e.preventDefault();
+1188|        this.insertNewline();
+1189|        return;
+1190|      }
+1191|
+1192|      // Regular character typing
+1193|      if (e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey) {
+1194|        e.preventDefault();
+1195|        this.insertText(e.key, true);
+1196|        return;
+1197|      }
+1198|    }
+1199|
+1200|    _handleCopy(e) {
+1201|      const text = this.getSelectedText();
+1202|      if (!text) return;
+1203|      e.preventDefault();
+1204|      if (e.clipboardData) {
+1205|        e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', text);
+1206|      }
+1207|    }
+1208|
+1209|    _handleCut(e) {
+1210|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+1211|      const text = this.getSelectedText();
+1212|      if (!text) return;
+1213|      e.preventDefault();
+1214|      if (e.clipboardData) {
+1215|        e.clipboardData.setData('text/plain', text);
+1216|      }
+1217|      this.deleteBackward();
+1218|    }
+1219|
+1220|    _handlePaste(e) {
+1221|      if (this.options.readOnly) return;
+1222|      e.preventDefault();
+1223|      const text = e.clipboardData ? e.clipboardData.getData('text/plain')
: '';
+1224|      if (text) {
+1225|        this.insertText(text, false);
+1226|      }
+1227|    }
+1228|  }

**Reviewing the Editor View Implementation**

Okay, let's dive into the `editor-view.js` file. I need to make sure I have a 
solid understanding of how things are structured. First, I'm going to review the
core methods. I see:

1.  `_initDom`: It initializes the necessary DOM elements – the gutter, 
viewport, and all the layers for selections, search matches, lines, and carets. 
Good, that's the setup.
2.  `setText`: This sets the document text and importantly, resets the single 
caret to (0,0). Makes sense for initial loading or clearing the editor.
3.  Then we have all the text manipulation methods: `insertText`, 
`deleteBackward`, `deleteForward`, `insertNewline`, `indent`, `unindent`, and 
the history management ones: `undo`, `redo`, and `restoreRevisionContent`. These
are the bread and butter of text editing functionality.
4.  Next, the movement and selection methods: `moveLeft`, `moveRight`, `moveUp`,
`moveDown`, `moveHome`, `moveEnd`, `selectAll`, `selectLine`, `selectWord`, and 
`selectMatch`. Gotta make sure the selection logic is flawless.
5.  `scrollCursorIntoView`: Essential for ensuring the user always sees the 
cursor within the viewport.
6.  Finally, event handling: click, drag, double click, triple click, and gutter
clicks. And, of course, the keyboard shortcuts; the ones everyone expects to 
work: Ctrl+A, Ctrl+Z, Ctrl+Y, Ctrl+F, Ctrl+H, Ctrl+S, Tab, Shift+Tab, Escape, 
Arrows, Home, End, PageUp, PageDown, Backspace, Delete, Enter, and typing. Plus 
the standard copy, cut, and paste.

Now, before I start integrating this, I need to test all of these methods 
comprehensively. I'll need to run this in Node with `jsdom` to simulate a 
browser environment and catch any errors. That way, I can test all methods 
execute without errors. I need to make sure there are no unexpected crashes or 
syntax bugs.

Alright, time to write a serious unit test suite. I have a plan:

*   **Grapheme segmentation:** Gotta ensure the editor handles complex 
characters correctly; emojis, surrogate pairs, and accented characters are 
critical to get right.
*   **DocumentModel:** Testing all the text insertion/deletion/retrieval 
functions and the document transformations. Indentation and word boundary logic 
are key.
*   **SelectionManager:** Single caret, multi-caret, merging, normalizing 
selections – this has to be rock solid.
*   **HistoryManager:** Coalescing typing events, undo, redo, and clearing 
history.
*   **FindController:** Need to test searching, next/prev navigation, wrapping, 
and the replace functionality.
*   **EditorView:** Finally, the full integration tests: typing, backspace, 
multi-caret editing, undo/redo, and making sure the revision restore works as 
expected.

to be a comprehensive test suite.

    const assert = require('assert');
    const { JSDOM } = require('jsdom');
    
    // Setup JSDOM environment
    const dom = new JSDOM(`<!DOCTYPE html><html><body><div 
id="editor-container"></div></body></html>`, {
      url: 'http://localhost:3000'
    });
    global.window = dom.window;
    global.document = dom.window.document;
    global.navigator = dom.window.navigator;
    global.HTMLCanvasElement = dom.window.HTMLCanvasElement;
    
    // Mock measureText for Canvas in JSDOM
    dom.window.HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.getContext = function() {
      return {
        measureText: function(str) {
          return { width: (str ? str.length : 0) * 8.4 };
        },
        font: ''
      };
    };
    
    // Load modules
    require('../public/js/grapheme.js');
    require('../public/js/document-model.js');
    require('../public/js/selection.js');
    require('../public/js/history.js');
    require('../public/js/find-controller.js');
    require('../public/js/editor-view.js');
    
    const Grapheme = window.GraphemeUtils;
    const DocumentModel = window.DocumentModel;
    const Selection = window.Selection;
    const SelectionManager = window.SelectionManager;
    const HistoryManager = window.HistoryManager;
    const FindController = window.FindController;
    const EditorView = window.EditorView;
    
    console.log('Running PatchPad unit tests...');
    
    // 1. Grapheme tests
    {
      const text = 'Hello 🚀👩‍👩‍👧‍👦 World!';
      const graphemes = Grapheme.getGraphemes(text);
      assert(graphemes.length > 0, 'Graphemes extracted');
      assert(graphemes.includes('🚀'), 'Emoji recognized');
      console.log('✔ Grapheme segmentation tests passed');
    }
    
    // 2. DocumentModel tests
    {
      const doc = new DocumentModel('Line 1\nLine 2\nLine 3');
      assert.strictEqual(doc.lineCount, 3);
      assert.strictEqual(doc.getLine(0), 'Line 1');
    
      // Insert test
      doc.insertAt({ row: 0, col: 4 }, ' inserted');
      assert.strictEqual(doc.getLine(0), 'Line inserted 1');
    
      // Multiline insert
      doc.insertAt({ row: 1, col: 4 }, ' A\nLine 2.5');
      assert.strictEqual(doc.lineCount, 4);
    
      // Delete range
      doc.deleteRange({ row: 0, col: 0 }, { row: 0, col: 5 });
      assert.strictEqual(doc.getLine(0), 'inserted 1');
    
      // Word boundaries
      const doc2 = new DocumentModel('The quick brown_fox jumps!');
      const w1 = doc2.findWordBoundaries(0, 5);
      assert.strictEqual(doc2.getRangeText({ row: 0, col: w1.startCol }, { row: 
0, col: w1.endCol }), 'quick');
    
      const nextW = doc2.findNextWord(0, 0);
      assert.strictEqual(nextW.col, 4); // start of 'quick'
    
      const prevW = doc2.findPrevWord(0, 10);
      assert.strictEqual(prevW.col, 4); // start of 'quick'
    
      console.log('✔ DocumentModel tests passed');
    }
    
    // 3. SelectionManager tests
    {
      const sm = new SelectionManager();
      sm.setSingleCaret(0, 0);
      assert.strictEqual(sm.selections.length, 1);
    
      // Add multi carets
      sm.addCaret(1, 5);
      sm.addCaret(2, 10);
      assert.strictEqual(sm.selections.length, 3);
    
      // Duplicate caret should not duplicate
      sm.normalize();
      assert.strictEqual(sm.selections.length, 3);
    
      console.log('✔ SelectionManager tests passed');
    }
    
    // 4. HistoryManager tests
    {
      const hm = new HistoryManager();
      const sel = [new Selection({ row: 0, col: 0 }, { row: 0, col: 0 })];
    
      hm.recordChange({
        beforeText: 'abc',
        afterText: 'abcd',
        beforeSelections: sel,
        afterSelections: sel,
        actionType: 'type_char',
        caretPos: { row: 0, col: 3 }
      });
    
      // Coalesce typing
      hm.recordChange({
        beforeText: 'abcd',
        afterText: 'abcde',
        beforeSelections: sel,
        afterSelections: sel,
        actionType: 'type_char',
        caretPos: { row: 0, col: 4 }
      });
    
      assert.strictEqual(hm.undoStack.length, 1, 'Typing coalesced into single 
action');
    
      const undone = hm.undo('abcde');
      assert.strictEqual(undone.text, 'abc', 'Undo returned initial text');
    
      const redone = hm.redo('abc');
      assert.strictEqual(redone.text, 'abcde', 'Redo returned final text');
    
      console.log('✔ HistoryManager tests passed');
    }
    
    // 5. FindController tests
    {
      const doc = new DocumentModel('alpha beta alpha gamma ALPHA');
      const fc = new FindController();
      fc.setQuery('alpha');
      const res = fc.findMatches(doc);
      assert.strictEqual(res.count, 3, 'Found 3 case-insensitive matches');
    
      const next = fc.nextMatch();
      assert(next !== null);
    
      console.log('✔ FindController tests passed');
    }
    
    // 6. EditorView end-to-end tests
    {
      const container = document.getElementById('editor-container');
      const editor = new EditorView(container);
    
      editor.setText('Hello world');
      assert.strictEqual(editor.getText(), 'Hello world');
    
      // Type single char
      editor.insertText('!', true);
      assert.strictEqual(editor.getText(), '!Hello world');
    
      // Test undo
      editor.undo();
      assert.strictEqual(editor.getText(), 'Hello world');
    
      // Test redo
      editor.redo();
      assert.strictEqual(editor.getText(), '!Hello world');
    
      // Test Restore revision as undoable unsaved action
      editor.restoreRevisionContent('Restored Revision Content');
      assert.strictEqual(editor.getText(), 'Restored Revision Content');
      assert.strictEqual(editor.isDirty, true);
    
      // Undo the restore
      editor.undo();
      assert.strictEqual(editor.getText(), '!Hello world');
    
      console.log('✔ EditorView tests passed');
    }
    
    console.log('\nAll unit tests passed successfully!');

Reasoning:
**My Design Blueprint: Crafting a Robust Document Editor**

Okay, here's my plan. I'm going to build a comprehensive document editor, 
focusing on a clean and functional user experience. I'm thinking modularity and 
maintainability from the get-go.

First, I'll establish a solid foundation with my API service in 
`public/js/api.js`. This will handle all the heavy lifting: fetching, saving, 
and managing revisions. I need this to be robust, gracefully handling conflicts 
(409 errors) and general errors.

Next, I'll tackle the revision history component with 
`public/js/revision-controller.js`. This will house the sidebar for browsing 
revisions, the diff/preview viewer for comparison, and the core restore logic.

Then comes the main application controller, `public/js/app.js`. This is where it
all comes together. The UI will have a prominent header displaying the document 
title, author, status tags (Saved, Unsaved Changes, Conflict), and revision 
information. I'll include a save state badge and cursor position.

The toolbar will offer crucial functionality. Save with a shortcut, undo, redo, 
and a find & replace toggle. There will be a revision history toggle, and a 
document selector/reports modal to choose available documents. Font size 
controls are a must, too.

The Find & Replace feature will be a floating bar that's easy to use. I'll 
implement find input with key bindings to keep the user experience seamless, a 
match counter, prev/next buttons, replace input, replace all button, and case 
sensitivity, whole word, regex options, and a close button.

I'll handle conflicts with a dedicated modal or banner. Clear explanations are 
key, along with clear options: keeping the current draft (with export/copy), 
reloading the server version, or comparing with the server version.

For the revision preview, I'll use a banner and side panel, showing that the 
revisions are read-only previews. Restoring a revision will load it into the 
editor as an unsaved action.

Finally, a status bar will show Document status, line/column position, caret, 
character counts, and a save timestamp.

For the visual aspect, I'll craft a `public/css/editor.css` file with a clean 
and professional UI style. I'm thinking a dark/neutral theme, high contrast, 
readable typography, and responsive design for 1280x800 resolution and beyond.

And of course, `public/index.html` will provide the semantic HTML structure.

Let's start building that API service.

    // API Client for PatchPad
    (function(window) {
      'use strict';
    
      class ApiClient {
        constructor(baseUrl = '') {
          this.baseUrl = baseUrl;
        }
    
        async listDocuments() {
          const res = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/documents`);
          if (!res.ok) {
            throw new Error(`Failed to list documents: ${res.statusText}`);
          }
          return await res.json();
        }
    
        async getDocument(documentId) {
          const res = await 
fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}`);
          if (!res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            throw new Error(data.error || `Failed to fetch document: 
${res.statusText}`);
          }
          return await res.json();
        }
    
        async getRevisions(documentId) {
          const res = await 
fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}/revisions
`);
          if (!res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            throw new Error(data.error || `Failed to fetch revisions: 
${res.statusText}`);
          }
          return await res.json();
        }
    
        async getRevision(documentId, revisionNum) {
          const res = await 
fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}/revisions
/${revisionNum}`);
          if (!res.ok) {
            const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
            throw new Error(data.error || `Failed to fetch revision 
${revisionNum}: ${res.statusText}`);
          }
          return await res.json();
        }
    
        async saveDocument({ documentId, baseRevision, content, author, summary 
}) {
          const res = await 
fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/documents/${encodeURIComponent(documentId)}/save`, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
              'Content-Type': 'application/json'
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              documentId,
              baseRevision,
              content,
              author,
              summary
            })
          });
    
          const data = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    
          if (res.status === 409) {
            return {
              conflict: true,
              status: 'conflict',
              currentRevision: data.currentRevision,
              currentContent: data.currentContent,
              message: data.message || 'Conflict: Document has been modified by 
another session'
            };
          }
    
          if (!res.ok) {
            return {
              error: true,
              status: res.status,
              message: data.error || data.message || `Save failed with status 
${res.status}`
            };
          }
    
          return {
            success: true,
            status: data.status,
            revision: data.revision,
            timestamp: data.timestamp,
            message: data.message
          };
        }
      }
    
      window.ApiClient = ApiClient;
    })(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : global);
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create revision-controller.js for history panel and previewing

Reasoning:
**My Plan for the Revision Controller**

Okay, time to get this revision history functionality built out. I'm going to 
create `public/js/revision-controller.js` and I need to figure out the scope of 
what I'm implementing. First, I need to fetch the revision data from the server 
using `api.getRevisions(documentId)`. That gives me the raw data to work with.

Then, I need to render a list of these revisions in the sidebar or drawer. This 
list needs to include a few key pieces of information for each revision: the 
revision number (with a "Latest" or "Current" tag on the most recent), a nicely 
formatted timestamp (including both absolute and relative time like "Sep 11, 
2026, 1:15 PM" and "2 hours ago"), the author, a summary, and the actions. The 
actions should be "Preview" and "Restore" buttons (the "Restore" button 
obviously only appearing if it's not the current version).

Preview mode is where it gets interesting. I'll need to fetch the full text for 
a selected revision using `api.getRevision(documentId, rev)`. I'll want to 
display a clear banner that says "Previewing Revision X (saved on ... by ...)" 
to remove any ambiguity. Inside the preview mode, there'll be a "Restore this 
revision" button and an "Exit preview" button. Critically, before I switch into 
preview mode, I need to make sure to save the user's current editor draft in 
memory. This is non-negotiable. If the user exits preview mode, it should 
restore the draft without losing anything.

If the user clicks "Restore this revision," I need to do the following: restore 
the revision content into the active editor, making it an *unsaved* editor 
action so that it appears on the undo stack; then, exit preview mode, and set 
the editor state to "dirty" (i.e. unsaved).

Finally, I can't forget about formatting timestamps properly. I'll utilize the 
Date formatting capabilities here to make sure they're human-readable and 
consistent. Now, let's start implementing this in 
`public/js/revision-controller.js`.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/js/revision-controller.js"
  file_text: 
    // Revision History Controller
    (function(window) {
      'use strict';
    
      class RevisionController {
        constructor(api, editorView, containerElement, options = {}) {
          this.api = api;
          this.editor = editorView;
          this.container = containerElement;
          this.options = Object.assign({
            onPreviewStart: null,
            onPreviewEnd: null,
            onRestore: null,
            documentId: 'incident-alpha'
          }, options);
    
          this.revisions = [];
          this.currentDocumentRevision = 1;
          this.previewingRevision = null;
          this.savedDraftBeforePreview = null;
          this.isOpen = false;
        }
    
        setDocumentId(id, currentRevision) {
          this.options.documentId = id;
          this.currentDocumentRevision = currentRevision;
          this.loadRevisions();
        }
    
        async loadRevisions() {
          if (!this.options.documentId) return;
          try {
            this.container.classList.add('loading');
            this.revisions = await 
this.api.getRevisions(this.options.documentId);
            this.render();
          } catch (err) {
            this.container.innerHTML = `<div class="history-error">Failed to 
load revision history: ${err.message}</div>`;
          } finally {
            this.container.classList.remove('loading');
          }
        }
    
        formatTimestamp(isoString) {
          try {
            const d = new Date(isoString);
            return d.toLocaleString(undefined, {
              month: 'short',
              day: 'numeric',
              year: 'numeric',
              hour: '2-digit',
              minute: '2-digit',
              second: '2-digit'
            });
          } catch (e) {
            return isoString;
          }
        }
    
        render() {
          if (this.revisions.length === 0) {
            this.container.innerHTML = `<div class="history-empty">No revisions 
recorded yet.</div>`;
            return;
          }
    
          let html = `<div class="history-list">`;
          for (const rev of this.revisions) {
            const isCurrent = rev.revision === this.currentDocumentRevision;
            const isPreviewing = this.previewingRevision === rev.revision;
    
            html += `
              <div class="history-item${isCurrent ? ' current' : 
''}${isPreviewing ? ' previewing' : ''}" data-revision="${rev.revision}">
                <div class="history-item-header">
                  <div class="history-rev-badge">Rev ${rev.revision}</div>
                  ${isCurrent ? '<span class="history-tag 
current-tag">Active</span>' : ''}
                  ${isPreviewing ? '<span class="history-tag 
preview-tag">Previewing</span>' : ''}
                  <span 
class="history-timestamp">${this.formatTimestamp(rev.timestamp)}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="history-item-meta">
                  <span class="history-author">👤 ${rev.author || 
'Anonymous'}</span>
                  <span class="history-chars">${rev.charCount ? 
rev.charCount.toLocaleString() + ' chars' : ''}</span>
                </div>
                ${rev.summary ? `<div 
class="history-summary">${rev.summary}</div>` : ''}
                <div class="history-actions">
                  ${isPreviewing ? `
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary btn-exit-preview" 
data-revision="${rev.revision}">Exit Preview</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary btn-restore-rev" 
data-revision="${rev.revision}">Restore</button>
                  ` : `
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary btn-preview-rev" 
data-revision="${rev.revision}">Preview</button>
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline btn-restore-rev" 
data-revision="${rev.revision}">Restore</button>
                  `}
                </div>
              </div>
            `;
          }
          html += `</div>`;
          this.container.innerHTML = html;
          this._bindItemEvents();
        }
    
        _bindItemEvents() {
          const previewBtns = 
this.container.querySelectorAll('.btn-preview-rev');
          previewBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
              const revNum = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-revision'), 10);
              this.startPreview(revNum);
            });
          });
    
          const exitBtns = this.container.querySelectorAll('.btn-exit-preview');
          exitBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
              this.exitPreview();
            });
          });
    
          const restoreBtns = 
this.container.querySelectorAll('.btn-restore-rev');
          restoreBtns.forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
              const revNum = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-revision'), 10);
              this.restoreRevision(revNum);
            });
          });
        }
    
        async startPreview(revNum) {
          if (this.previewingRevision === null) {
            // Save current draft text and history state
            this.savedDraftBeforePreview = {
              text: this.editor.getText(),
              selections: this.editor.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone())
            };
          }
    
          try {
            const revData = await this.api.getRevision(this.options.documentId, 
revNum);
            this.previewingRevision = revNum;
            this.editor.options.readOnly = true;
            this.editor.setText(revData.content, false);
            this.render();
    
            if (this.options.onPreviewStart) {
              this.options.onPreviewStart(revData);
            }
          } catch (err) {
            alert(`Failed to preview revision ${revNum}: ${err.message}`);
          }
        }
    
        exitPreview() {
          if (this.previewingRevision === null) return;
          this.editor.options.readOnly = false;
          this.previewingRevision = null;
    
          if (this.savedDraftBeforePreview) {
            this.editor.setText(this.savedDraftBeforePreview.text, false);
            this.editor.selectionManager.selections = 
this.savedDraftBeforePreview.selections;
            this.savedDraftBeforePreview = null;
          }
    
          this.render();
          if (this.options.onPreviewEnd) {
            this.options.onPreviewEnd();
          }
        }
    
        async restoreRevision(revNum) {
          try {
            let revContent;
            if (this.previewingRevision === revNum) {
              revContent = this.editor.getText();
            } else {
              const revData = await 
this.api.getRevision(this.options.documentId, revNum);
              revContent = revData.content;
            }
    
            // Exit preview mode first
            this.editor.options.readOnly = false;
            this.previewingRevision = null;
            this.savedDraftBeforePreview = null;
    
            // Restore content into editor as unsaved action
            this.editor.restoreRevisionContent(revContent);
            this.render();
    
            if (this.options.onRestore) {
              this.options.onRestore(revNum);
            }
          } catch (err) {
            alert(`Failed to restore revision ${revNum}: ${err.message}`);
          }
        }
      }
    
      window.RevisionController = RevisionController;
    })(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : global);
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

+1|// Revision History Controller
+2|(function(window) {
+3|  'use strict';
 4|
+5|  class RevisionController {
+6|    constructor(api, editorView, containerElement, options = {}) {
+7|      this.api = api;
+8|      this.editor = editorView;
+9|      this.container = containerElement;
+10|      this.options = Object.assign({
+11|        onPreviewStart: null,
+12|        onPreviewEnd: null,
+13|        onRestore: null,
+14|        documentId: 'incident-alpha'
+15|      }, options);
+16|
+17|      this.revisions = [];
+18|      this.currentDocumentRevision = 1;
+19|      this.previewingRevision = null;
+20|      this.savedDraftBeforePreview = null;
+21|      this.isOpen = false;
+22|    }
+23|
+24|    setDocumentId(id, currentRevision) {
+25|      this.options.documentId = id;
+26|      this.currentDocumentRevision = currentRevision;
+27|      this.loadRevisions();
+28|    }
+29|
+30|    async loadRevisions() {
+31|      if (!this.options.documentId) return;
+32|      try {
+33|        this.container.classList.add('loading');
+34|        this.revisions = await 
this.api.getRevisions(this.options.documentId);
+35|        this.render();
+36|      } catch (err) {
+37|        this.container.innerHTML = `<div class="history-error">Failed to 
load revision history: ${err.message}</div>`;
+38|      } finally {
+39|        this.container.classList.remove('loading');
+40|      }
+41|    }
+42|
+43|    formatTimestamp(isoString) {
+44|      try {
+45|        const d = new Date(isoString);
+46|        return d.toLocaleString(undefined, {
+47|          month: 'short',
+48|          day: 'numeric',
+49|          year: 'numeric',
+50|          hour: '2-digit',
+51|          minute: '2-digit',
+52|          second: '2-digit'
+53|        });
+54|      } catch (e) {
+55|        return isoString;
+56|      }
+57|    }
+58|
+59|    render() {
+60|      if (this.revisions.length === 0) {
+61|        this.container.innerHTML = `<div class="history-empty">No revisions 
recorded yet.</div>`;
+62|        return;
+63|      }
+64|
+65|      let html = `<div class="history-list">`;
+66|      for (const rev of this.revisions) {
+67|        const isCurrent = rev.revision === this.currentDocumentRevision;
+68|        const isPreviewing = this.previewingRevision === rev.revision;
+69|
+70|        html += `
+71|          <div class="history-item${isCurrent ? ' current' : 
''}${isPreviewing ? ' previewing' : ''}" data-revision="${rev.revision}">
+72|            <div class="history-item-header">
+73|              <div class="history-rev-badge">Rev ${rev.revision}</div>
+74|              ${isCurrent ? '<span class="history-tag 
current-tag">Active</span>' : ''}
+75|              ${isPreviewing ? '<span class="history-tag 
preview-tag">Previewing</span>' : ''}
+76|              <span 
class="history-timestamp">${this.formatTimestamp(rev.timestamp)}</span>
+77|            </div>
+78|            <div class="history-item-meta">
+79|              <span class="history-author">👤 ${rev.author || 
'Anonymous'}</span>
+80|              <span class="history-chars">${rev.charCount ? 
rev.charCount.toLocaleString() + ' chars' : ''}</span>
+81|            </div>
+82|            ${rev.summary ? `<div 
class="history-summary">${rev.summary}</div>` : ''}
+83|            <div class="history-actions">
+84|              ${isPreviewing ? `
+85|                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary btn-exit-preview" 
data-revision="${rev.revision}">Exit Preview</button>
+86|                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary btn-restore-rev" 
data-revision="${rev.revision}">Restore</button>
+87|              ` : `
+88|                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-secondary btn-preview-rev" 
data-revision="${rev.revision}">Preview</button>
+89|                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline btn-restore-rev" 
data-revision="${rev.revision}">Restore</button>
+90|              `}
+91|            </div>
+92|          </div>
+93|        `;
+94|      }
+95|      html += `</div>`;
+96|      this.container.innerHTML = html;
+97|      this._bindItemEvents();
+98|    }
+99|
+100|    _bindItemEvents() {
+101|      const previewBtns = 
this.container.querySelectorAll('.btn-preview-rev');
+102|      previewBtns.forEach(btn => {
+103|        btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+104|          const revNum = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-revision'), 10);
+105|          this.startPreview(revNum);
+106|        });
+107|      });
+108|
+109|      const exitBtns = 
this.container.querySelectorAll('.btn-exit-preview');
+110|      exitBtns.forEach(btn => {
+111|        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
+112|          this.exitPreview();
+113|        });
+114|      });
+115|
+116|      const restoreBtns = 
this.container.querySelectorAll('.btn-restore-rev');
+117|      restoreBtns.forEach(btn => {
+118|        btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
+119|          const revNum = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-revision'), 10);
+120|          this.restoreRevision(revNum);
+121|        });
+122|      });
+123|    }
+124|
+125|    async startPreview(revNum) {
+126|      if (this.previewingRevision === null) {
+127|        // Save current draft text and history state
+128|        this.savedDraftBeforePreview = {
+129|          text: this.editor.getText(),
+130|          selections: this.editor.selectionManager.selections.map(s => 
s.clone())
+131|        };
+132|      }
+133|
+134|      try {
+135|        const revData = await this.api.getRevision(this.options.documentId,
revNum);
+136|        this.previewingRevision = revNum;
+137|        this.editor.options.readOnly = true;
+138|        this.editor.setText(revData.content, false);
+139|        this.render();
+140|
+141|        if (this.options.onPreviewStart) {
+142|          this.options.onPreviewStart(revData);
+143|        }
+144|      } catch (err) {
+145|        alert(`Failed to preview revision ${revNum}: ${err.message}`);
+146|      }
+147|    }
+148|
+149|    exitPreview() {
+150|      if (this.previewingRevision === null) return;
+151|      this.editor.options.readOnly = false;
+152|      this.previewingRevision = null;
+153|
+154|      if (this.savedDraftBeforePreview) {
+155|        this.editor.setText(this.savedDraftBeforePreview.text, false);
+156|        this.editor.selectionManager.selections = 
this.savedDraftBeforePreview.selections;
+157|        this.savedDraftBeforePreview = null;
+158|      }
+159|
+160|      this.render();
+161|      if (this.options.onPreviewEnd) {
+162|        this.options.onPreviewEnd();
+163|      }
+164|    }
+165|
+166|    async restoreRevision(revNum) {
+167|      try {
+168|        let revContent;
+169|        if (this.previewingRevision === revNum) {
+170|          revContent = this.editor.getText();
+171|        } else {
+172|          const revData = await 
this.api.getRevision(this.options.documentId, revNum);
+173|          revContent = revData.content;
+174|        }
+175|
+176|        // Exit preview mode first
+177|        this.editor.options.readOnly = false;
+178|        this.previewingRevision = null;
+179|        this.savedDraftBeforePreview = null;
+180|
+181|        // Restore content into editor as unsaved action
+182|        this.editor.restoreRevisionContent(revContent);
+183|        this.render();
+184|
+185|        if (this.options.onRestore) {
+186|          this.options.onRestore(revNum);
+187|        }
+188|      } catch (err) {
+189|        alert(`Failed to restore revision ${revNum}: ${err.message}`);
+190|      }
+191|    }
+192|  }
+193|
+194|  window.RevisionController = RevisionController;
+195|})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : global);
+196|

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create app.js UI controller

Reasoning:
**UI Component Coordination for Northwind API Incident Report**

Okay, let's dive into designing `public/js/app.js` to orchestrate this UI. 
First, I need to keep the requirements top of mind.

For the interface layout and header, it's crucial to maintain the report title, 
"Northwind API Incident Report," so the user always knows what they're working 
with. I'll need to display the author ("Riley Stone"), Document ID 
("incident-alpha"), Status ("Draft for executive review"), and a Summary. For 
status indicators, I'll need: Save Status: "Saved" (green dot) / "Unsaved 
changes" (amber dot) / "Saving..." (spinner/blue) / "Conflict" (red dot). Also, 
the Revision, "Rev 1" (or the current revision number). Cursor, displaying "Ln 
1, Col 1" and the selection count, and Stats, "1,226 lines", "159,334 chars".

The toolbar will need specific buttons: Save (with Ctrl+S shortcut), Undo 
(Ctrl+Z) - which I'll disable if `!editor.history.canUndo()`, Redo (Ctrl+Y) - 
disabled if `!editor.history.canRedo()`, Find & Replace (Ctrl+F / Ctrl+H), a 
Revision History button with a revision count badge, a Documents/Reports button 
(that opens a modal displaying available reports by id, title, and author), and 
Font Size (+ / -) controls.

The Find & Replace bar requires careful handling. The Find input must update the
match count as the user types, and Enter should trigger Find Next, Shift+Enter, 
Find Prev, and Escape returns focus to the editor without changing the selection
(that's crucial!). The matches count badge needs to show "Match 1 of 12" or "No 
matches" or "3 matches." It should have Prev and Next buttons. The Replace input
should have Enter replacing next. There will be a Replace Current button and a 
Replace All button and it needs to display a notification. Toggle buttons for 
Case Sensitive (`Aa`), Whole Word (`\b`), and Regex (`.*`) are also required, 
along with a Close button. Also, it needs to have the Escape key inside the 
editor focus the Find input.

Conflict handling is critical. When the save returns a 409 Conflict, I need to 
update the status to "Conflict" and preserve the user's unsaved draft. 
Crucially, I'll show a clear Conflict Modal. This modal needs to explain that 
the server version has been updated to revision X, and that the local draft is 
still preserved. There should be three options: "Keep Draft (close dialog, 
continue editing or copy text)", "Reload Latest from Server (discard draft and 
load revision X)", and "Copy Draft to Clipboard".

I also need to create a Revision Preview mode. A top banner "Previewing Revision
X (saved on ...). Editing is disabled." must be present. There will be a 
"Restore this revision" button, and a "Close preview" button.

Finally, the Available Reports Modal will fetch from `/api/documents`. It needs 
to display a list of documents with their id, title, author, summary, 
currentRevision, and updatedAt, and allow the user to open a selected document.

Right, now I'm ready to start writing `public/js/app.js`.

  command: "create"
